## Constructed surface and represented cases

All new fully qualified names are under `YulangClosure.RustInventoryRequestR63`.
`Owner` holds complete typed primitive R55/R57 inventories, source Catch records
and all seven R61 dependency forms; Replay keeps both parents and their order.
`source`, `base`, `native` assemble the existing models from those fields.
Relation endpoint payloads are retained; state/index use R61's vector projection.
`internal` derives registration/Lambda/annotation/row from plan/owner-component
construction/permission reads. `catchFact` selects the retained source Catch's
scrutinee, then its collected slot51 fact. `target` enumerates every owner view
member for the operation/declaration/family and requires a singleton. Caller
coordinates contain owner identity and source use/Catch/provider occurrences.

Entry retains raw entryRow; Catch uses the selected fact's canonicalLower.
Both target the derived provider endpoint. The total `terminal` function has a
default only when target is absent: extraction checks isSome and
`constructed_terminal_checked` proves that a successful package cannot use the
default. Raw/canonical facts and distinct semantic/collected namespaces remain.

Coverage/lookup/request equations are `source_inventory_equations`,
`native_inventory_equations`, `permission_lookup`, `emission_lookup`,
`all_permissions_retained`, `all_provider_competitors_retained`,
`ordered_dependency_equations`, `internal_request_equation`,
`constructed_request_coordinates`, `canonical_entry_from_owner`,
`raw_entry_is_not_replaced`, `target_from_complete_provider_inventory`,
`catch_request_namespace`, `constructed_internal_fact_namespace`.

`concrete_package_is_constructed` discharges a finite package and
`concrete_constructed_first_entry` computes .ok(some2) on that same package.
Discriminators retain Entry raw4/canonical40, Catch raw6/canonical60, and both
semantic(store2,index88) and collected(batch3,index88). Foreign state with the
same batch, missing/duplicate permissions, missing Catch fact, duplicate views
and a DISTINCT matching provider view reject. Universal inventory equations
retain all competitors, not only fixture members. R55/R57 primitive constructors
and every R61 dependency parent case remain represented.
